{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-haiku-4-5-20251001","id":"msg_0159CFRJXBofL9uYoiS5bmt9","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_0146btrschkhxKqY9kf4VFMF","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/logs/agent/onboarding.md","content":"# Grafana Codebase Architecture Onboarding\n\n## 1. Main Entry Points\n\n### Binary Entry Point\nThe main Grafana server binary is built from `pkg/cmd/grafana/main.go` (lines 23-31). The application uses the **urfave/cli v2** framework for command-line parsing and routing.\n\n**Bootstrap Flow:**\n1. The grafana-server command delegates to the main `grafana` binary via `pkg/util/cmd/cmd.go:RunGrafanaCmd()` (lines 15-74)\n2. The main CLI app is created in `pkg/cmd/grafana/main.go:MainApp()` (lines 34-73), which registers two CLI commands:\n   - `CLICommand`: For grafana-cli utilities\n   - `ServerCommand`: For starting the server\n\n3. The server command is defined in `pkg/cmd/grafana-server/commands/cli.go:ServerCommand()` (lines 28-44)\n4. The `RunServer()` function (lines 46-125) performs the bootstrap:\n   - Reads configuration from `setting.NewCfgFromArgs()` (lines 96-101)\n   - Initializes profiling and tracing (lines 71-76)\n   - Calls `server.Initialize()` from `pkg/server/server.go` (lines 108-120)\n   - Sets up signal handling for graceful shutdown (line 123)\n   - Runs the HTTP server (line 124)\n\n### Service Initialization\nThe `server.Initialize()` function uses **Google Wire** dependency injection (defined in `pkg/server/wire.go`) to instantiate all core services. The Wire configuration includes 50+ services covering APIs, datasources, authentication, provisioning, and more (wire.go lines 39-443).\n\n## 2. Core Backend Packages\n\nGrafana's backend architecture is organized under `pkg/` with the following key packages:\n\n### 1. **pkg/api** - HTTP API and Routing\n- **Location**: `pkg/api/api.go` (HTTP routes registered at line 55)\n- **Responsibility**: Defines REST API endpoints, middleware, and request handlers\n- **Key Files**:\n  - `http_server.go`: HTTPServer struct and initialization\n  - `routing/`: Route registration and matching logic\n  - Handlers for dashboards, datasources, folders, users, plugins, etc.\n- **Pattern**: Each resource type has a handler file (e.g., `dashboard.go`, `datasources.go`)\n\n### 2. **pkg/services/datasources** - Data Source Management\n- **Location**: `pkg/services/datasources/`\n- **Responsibility**: Data source CRUD operations, caching, and service proxy\n- **Key Components**:\n  - `service/`: Main datasource service with cache implementation\n  - Integrates with `pkg/tsdb/` for backend plugin communication\n\n### 3. **pkg/tsdb** - Time Series Database Plugins\n- **Location**: `pkg/tsdb/`\n- **Responsibility**: Built-in data source drivers\n- **Built-in Datasources** (examples):\n  - `prometheus/`: Prometheus datasource (pkg/tsdb/prometheus/prometheus.go)\n  - `loki/`: Loki logs datasource\n  - `elasticsearch/`: Elasticsearch datasource\n  - `cloudwatch/`, `azuremonitor/`, `cloud-monitoring/`: Cloud integrations\n- **Pattern**: Each datasource implements the `backend.QueryDataHandler`, `backend.CheckHealthHandler`, and `backend.CallResourceHandler` interfaces from `grafana-plugin-sdk-go`\n\n### 4. **pkg/services/provisioning** - Configuration Provisioning\n- **Location**: `pkg/services/provisioning/`\n- **Responsibility**: Loads dashboards, datasources, plugins, and alerting rules from YAML files\n- **Key Components**:\n  - `provisioning.go` (lines 39-88): Main ProvisioningService\n  - `dashboards/`: Dashboard provisioning (reads YAML, syncs to DB)\n  - `datasources/`: Datasource provisioning\n  - `plugins/`: Plugin provisioning\n\n### 5. **pkg/services/authentication & Authorization**\n- **Location**: `pkg/services/auth/`, `pkg/services/authn/`, `pkg/services/accesscontrol/`\n- **Responsibility**: User authentication, identity providers, RBAC\n- **Components**:\n  - `auth/idimpl/`: ID token service\n  - `authn/authnimpl/`: Authentication service (supports OAuth, LDAP, SAML)\n  - `accesscontrol/acimpl/`: Access control and permission evaluation\n\n### 6. **pkg/registry** - Service Registry\n- **Location**: `pkg/registry/`\n- **Responsibility**: Registry pattern for discovering and registering background services\n- **Key Interfaces**:\n  - `BackgroundServiceRegistry`: Services that run in the background\n  - `UsageStatsProvidersRegistry`: Providers for usage statistics\n\n## 3. Datasource Plugin Architecture\n\n### Plugin System Overview\nGrafana supports two types of plugins:\n1. **Built-in datasources**: Compiled into the binary (under `pkg/tsdb/`)\n2. **Plugin datasources**: External processes communicating via gRPC or HTTP\n\n### Plugin Structure\n**Plugin Metadata** (`plugin.json`):\n- Located in `pkg/plugins/plugins.go:JSONData` (lines 87-100)\n- Defines plugin ID, type, name, category, and routing\n\n**Plugin Registry**:\n- Located in `pkg/plugins/manager/registry/in_memory.go`\n- In-memory registry stores all loaded plugins\n- Plugins registered via `registry.Add()` (lines 45-58)\n- Query plugins via `registry.Plugin()` (lines 29-31)\n\n### Backend Plugin Communication\n**grafana-plugin-sdk-go**:\n- Grafana uses `github.com/grafana/grafana-plugin-sdk-go/backend` interface\n- Built-in datasources implement these handlers:\n  - `QueryDataHandler`: Execute queries\n  - `CheckHealthHandler`: Health checks\n  - `CallResourceHandler`: Custom resource endpoints\n  - `AdmissionHandler`: Validate/mutate objects\n  - `StreamHandler`: Real-time streaming\n  - `CollectMetricsHandler`: Prometheus metrics\n\n**Example: Prometheus Datasource** (`pkg/tsdb/prometheus/prometheus.go`):\n```go\nfunc (s *Service) QueryData(ctx context.Context, req *backend.QueryDataRequest) (*backend.QueryDataResponse, error)\nfunc (s *Service) CheckHealth(ctx context.Context, req *backend.CheckHealthRequest) (*backend.CheckHealthResult, error)\n```\n\n### Plugin Loading\n**Plugin Manager**: `pkg/plugins/manager/`\n- `loader/loader.go`: Discovers and loads plugins from disk\n- `loader/finder/`: File system scanning to find plugin.json files\n- `sources/`: Plugin sources (bundled, marketplace, etc.)\n\n**Plugin Routing**:\n- External datasources route requests through `pkg/api/pluginproxy/pluginproxy.go`\n- Routes defined in `plugin.json` with HTTP method and path matching (pluginproxy.go:61-93)\n\n## 4. Dashboard Provisioning Pipeline\n\n### Provisioning Flow\nDashboards are provisioned through YAML configuration files. The flow is:\n\n**1. Configuration Loading**\n- Config location: `provisioning/dashboards/*.yml` (from `Cfg.ProvisioningPath`)\n- Read by `pkg/services/provisioning/dashboards/config_reader.go:cfgReader.readConfig()`\n- Parses config files to `config` struct with source definitions\n\n**2. File Discovery**\n- `dashboards/file_reader.go`: Walks disk to find dashboard JSON files\n- Monitors configured directories for changes\n- Supports symlinks and local file paths\n\n**3.
